TL;DR: 本文利用非平衡分子动力学(NEMD)研究铜-石墨烯-铜界面的热传输，发现晶格初始化策略等构型选择会导致Kapitza电阻相差两倍，且较低的残余应变会在界面附近形成阻尼边界层增大热阻，而声子谱重叠增大反而对应更高的Kapitza电阻，挑战了传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中NEMD模拟已识别出石墨烯-铜界面Kapitza电阻的趋势，但模拟结果和可靠性可能严重依赖于未充分探索的构型选择（如晶格初始化、石墨烯褶皱等）。

Method: 采用非平衡分子动力学(NEMD)方法，研究有限域长度和域构型（包括晶格初始化及伴随的石墨烯褶皱）对热导率和Kapitza电阻的影响。

Result: 1) 两种常规晶格初始化策略导致Kapitza电阻相差两倍；2) 较低的残余应变下声子谱重叠增大但Kapitza电阻反而更高；3) 低应变下界面附近形成阻尼边界层，增加局域结构无序和谱展宽；4) Kapitza电阻对域长度和边界温度不敏感，而铜晶格热导率呈现明显的尺寸和温度依赖性。

Conclusion: 界面热传输不能仅由声子谱重叠来表征；低残余应变下阻尼边界层的形成是增大热阻的关键机制；晶格初始化策略等构型选择对模拟结果的可靠性有显著影响。

Abstract: We study thermal transport across copper-graphene-copper interfaces using Non-Equilibrium Molecular Dynamics (NEMD), focusing on the influence of finite domain length and domain configuration, including lattice initialisation and associated graphene wrinkling, on the predicted thermal conductivity and Kapitza resistance. In the literature, NEMD simulations identified trends in the Kapitza resistance of graphene-copper interfaces. However, the simulation outcomes and reliability may depend heavily on configuration choices that are underexplored in the literature. We identify a strong sensitivity of the Kapitza resistance to domain configuration choices that affect the lattice constants and atomic density. We show that two conventional lattice initialisation strategies yield a factor of two difference in the Kapitza resistance, despite differences of only a few per cent in the lattice parameters. This behaviour is accompanied by strain-dependent shifts in the graphene and copper phonon spectra, and by increased phonon overlap at lower strain. Counter to conventional expectations, greater phonon-mode overlap coincides with higher Kapitza resistance, showing that spectral overlap alone cannot capture the interfacial heat-transfer dynamics. We suggest that in lattices initialised with lower residual strain, a damping boundary layer develops near the interface, and increases thermal resistance, as indicated by increased local structural disorder and local spectral broadening over a wider interfacial region. Beyond strain- and density-related effects associated with the lattice constants, Kapitza resistance shows no significant dependence on domain length or boundary temperature enforcement in this study. By contrast, the copper lattice conductivity exhibits clear domain-size and temperature dependence, consistent with phonon mean-free-path limitations and supported by phonon spectral analysis.

</details>


### [100] [Electrical Control of Altermagnetism in a Quasi-1D Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16856)
*Alberto M. Ruiz,Cuiju Yu,Diego López-Alcalá,Jose L. Lado,Adolfo O. Fumega,José J. Baldoví*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了准一维反铁磁链嵌入二维晶格是实现交错磁性的通用路径，通过第一性原理计算和自旋空间群分析，证明了在AgCrP₂S₆范德华磁体中，面外对称性破缺可产生非相对论性d波自旋分裂，且该分裂可被电场和Janus取代等调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁性在三维和二维交换作用系统中已被广泛研究，但在准一维磁体中的扩展几乎未被探索，因此有必要研究准一维系统中的交错磁性及其调控机制。

Method: 以实验已确立的AgCrP₂S₆范德华磁体为研究对象，结合第一性原理计算和自旋空间群分析，构建有效紧束缚模型，并探索电场、Janus取代和铁电界面工程等调控手段。

Result: 面外对称性破缺可产生非相对论性d波自旋分裂；该分裂随面外电场强度线性变化且随电场方向反转；紧束缚模型将交错磁响应归因于各向异性的第三近邻链间跳跃；Janus取代也可诱导d波自旋织构；与CuInP₂S₆铁电界面可实现对完全补偿亚铁磁态中自旋分裂带的极化调控。

Conclusion: 准一维反铁磁体可作为交错磁性的构建单元，为低维交错磁性材料的设计和调控提供了新路径。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is a collinear magnetic state characterized by momentum-dependent spin splitting in fully compensated materials. While widely investigated in systems governed by three- or two-dimensional exchange interactions, its extension to quasi-one-dimensional magnets remains almost unexplored. Focusing on the experimentally established AgCrP$_2$S$_6$ van der Waals magnet, we demonstrate that antiferromagnetic chains embedded in a two-dimensional lattice provide a general route to altermagnetism. Combining first-principles calculations and spin-space-group analysis, we show that out-of-plane symmetry breaking can generate a nonrelativistic d-wave spin splitting. An external out-of-plane electric field validates this mechanism, where the induced splitting increases linearly with field strength and reverses sign with field direction. We rationalize such behaviour by constructing an effective tight-binding model, which links the altermagnetic response to anisotropic third-neighbor interchain hoppings. Additionally, we show that Janus substitution also induces a d-wave spin texture, while ferroelectric interfacing with CuInP$_2$S$_6$ enables polarization-controlled spin-split bands in a fully compensated ferrimagnetic state. Our results establish quasi-one-dimensional antiferromagnets as building blocks for altermagnetism.

</details>


### [101] [Specimen design for material parameter identification using topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16865)
*Adeline Wihardja,Kaushik Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将贝叶斯最优实验设计与拓扑优化相结合的框架，用于自动设计最能获取材料本构模型信息的实验构型，实现建模与实验的强耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本构关系是连续介质力学的核心，但通常需要实验来确定参数。当前实验设计缺乏系统方法来自动识别哪些实验配置能提供最大信息量，导致实验效率低下。

Method: 将贝叶斯最优实验设计（最大化期望信息增益）与拓扑优化相结合，利用全场测量提供的高维数据，并通过当前模型的不确定性指导未来实验的几何设计。

Result: 提出了一种能够自动优化试件几何形状以最大化信息获取的框架，实现了实验设计与模型发现的双向驱动。

Conclusion: 通过强耦合建模与实验，该框架能够系统地设计出对本构模型发现最具信息量的实验配置，有望大幅提升材料参数辨识的效率与精度。

Abstract: Constitutive relations close the equations of continuum mechanics, and serve as a surrogate for a material in the design and engineering process. They are often specified in a parameterized form with parameters identified by experiment. In this paper, we propose a framework for identifying experimental configurations that are maximally informative for constitutive model discovery. The framework strongly couples modeling and experimentation: the model leverages high-dimensional data from full-field measurements, while the current uncertainty in the model guides the design of future experiments. We formulate this goal by integrating Bayesian optimal experimental design with topology optimization. The Bayesian design criterion quantifies expected information gain, which drives the topology optimization of the specimen geometry.

</details>


### [102] [DFT+U+V is equivalent to DFT+U with density-dependent hybridized projectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18071)
*Edward Linscott,Alberto Carta,Nicola Marzari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明，在V/U一阶近似下，DFT+U+V中的位间+V修正等价于在位DFT+U使用密度依赖的、与邻近位杂化的重新定义投影子。若投影子冻结，等价性部分失效。这为V如何影响共价性提供了明确机制，并重新审视了V的计算方法和Hubbard子空间的基本定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFT+U是处理局域d/f电子的常用方法，但位内修正倾向于过度局域化电荷并破坏共价键。位间+V修正被引入以解决此问题并已被广泛使用，但缺乏形式化的理论证明。本文旨在提供位间+V修正的形式化理论依据。

Method: 通过理论推导，证明在V/U一阶近似下，位间+V修正等价于在位DFT+U使用密度依赖的、与邻近位杂化的重新定义投影子。分析了投影子冻结与不冻结两种情形下的等价性差异。

Result: 在V/U一阶近似下，位间修正与在位DFT+U在密度依赖的、杂化后的投影子下严格等价；若投影子冻结（常见做法），则等价性部分失效。

Conclusion: 该发现为V如何影响共价性提供了显式机制，并提出了关于V应如何计算以及Hubbard子空间基本定义的更尖锐问题。

Abstract: Hubbard-corrected density-functional theory (DFT+$U$) is a popular tool for first-principles modeling of materials with localized $d$ or $f$ electrons, but its on-site corrections tend to over-localize charge and break covalent bonds. Inter-site $+V$ corrections were introduced to counter this and are now widely used, but a formal justification has been lacking. Here we show that -- to first order in $V/U$ -- inter-site corrections are exactly equivalent to on-site DFT+$U$ evaluated on a density-dependent redefinition of the Hubbard projectors, hybridized with those of neighboring sites, providing insight into the explicit mechanism by which $V$ affects covalency. If the projectors are held frozen, as is common practice, the equivalence partially breaks down. Beyond reinterpreting the formalism, these results sharpen the questions of how $V$ should be computed and what the Hubbard subspaces fundamentally are.

</details>


### [103] [Operando observation of strain relaxation in fatigued pearlitic steel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16867)
*Louis Lesage,Johan Ahlström,Yubin Zhang,Can Yıldırım*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用暗场X射线显微镜原位观察珠光体钢在退火过程中的晶粒尺度响应，发现渗碳体片层抑制了长程位错运动，但弹性应变分散在250°C以下即减少近40%，归因于片层间位错的短程湮灭，解释了铁路用珠光体钢在服役中的宏观软化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 珠光体钢在铁路车轮和轨道中广泛应用，但在循环载荷与摩擦热共同作用下会发生软化，导致疲劳失效。理解这一软化机制对于提升铁路材料寿命至关重要。

Method: 采用暗场X射线显微镜（DFXM）原位追踪疲劳珠光体钢在退火至550°C过程中的晶粒尺度响应，获取取向图和应变图。

Result: 取向图显示无显著晶格旋转和亚结构形成；应变图显示弹性应变分散收窄近40%，且恢复在250°C以下即开始。

Conclusion: 应变恢复归因于片层间位错的短程湮灭，将晶粒尺度的应变恢复与珠光体钢在服役中的宏观软化联系起来。

Abstract: Pearlitic steels are the material of choice for railway wheels and rails, where their lamellar ferrite-cementite structure balances cost, strength, and wear resistance. However, in use, cyclic loading combined with frictional heat softens the steel, promoting fatigue and eventual failure. Here we use Dark-Field X-ray Microscopy (DFXM) to follow, operando, the grain-scale response of a fatigued pearlitic colony during annealing up to 550 °C. Orientation maps reveal negligible lattice rotation and no sub-cell formation, indicating that the cementite lamellae suppress long-range dislocation motion. Strain maps nonetheless show pronounced relaxation: the elastic strain spread narrows by nearly 40%, with recovery starting below 250 °C, within the operating temperatures of railway wheels. We attribute this relaxation to short-range annihilation of dislocations confined between lamellae, linking grain-scale strain recovery to the macroscopic softening of pearlitic railway steels in service.

</details>


### [104] [Planar Microcavities can Suppress Exciplex Formation and Increase the Emission Efficiency of Organic Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16886)
*Tomohiro Ishii,Stéphane Kéna-Cohen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发现将ZnPc:TPBi共混薄膜置于法布里-珀罗光学微腔中，可显著抑制非辐射激基复合物的形成并消除双分子湮灭，使光致发光量子产率在连续波激发下提升超40倍。这一增强远超弱Purcell效应预期，源于腔体增强的Förster共振能量转移（FRET）从ZnPc单体向发光聚集体的转移，从而竞争过电荷转移至暗态激基复合物的路径。电磁计算预测FRET增强可达约400倍，与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学微腔广泛应用于调控有机半导体的自发辐射速率（Purcell效应），但其能否在辐射跃迁发生之前重塑分子激发态的形成路径（即调控分子间能量/电荷转移动力学）尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将ZnPc:TPBi共混膜嵌入平面法布里-珀罗微腔中，利用瞬态光谱、功率依赖光致发光测量和动力学建模分析激发态动力学，结合电磁理论计算预测FRET速率变化。

Result: 微腔使PLQY提高超40倍（CW激发下），归因于腔增强的FRET将能量从ZnPc单体高效转移至发光聚集体，抑制了向暗态激基复合物的电荷转移路径；理论预测FRET增强可达~400倍。

Conclusion: 光学微腔不仅能控制分子如何发光（辐射速率），还能调控分子形成哪些激发态（能量/电荷转移路径选择），为提升有机光电器件效率并降低效率滚降提供了新思路。

Abstract: Optical microcavities are widely used to control the emission of organic semiconductors, but their ability to reshape the molecular pathways that precede emission remains largely unexplored. Here we show that embedding a ZnPc:TPBi blend in a planar Fabry-Pérot microcavity suppresses the formation of non-radiative exciplexes and removes bimolecular annihilation at high excitation densities, increasing the photoluminescence quantum yield by more than forty-fold under continuous-wave excitation. This enhancement is far larger than expected from the weak Purcell effect. Instead, transient spectroscopy, power-dependent photoluminescence and kinetic modelling point to a cavity-induced rebalancing of excited-state populations: long-range Förster energy transfer from ZnPc monomers to emissive aggregates is enhanced, allowing it to outcompete charge transfer to dark exciplexes. Electromagnetic calculations predict FRET enhancements of up to ~400-fold at relevant distances, consistent with the observed suppression of exciplex-mediated losses. Our results show that optical cavities can control not only how molecules emit, but also which excited states they form, opening a route to improved efficiency and reduced roll-off in organic optoelectronic and photonic devices.

</details>


### [105] [The crystallographic quaternions and their product law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16899)
*Cyril Cayron*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将单位四元数直接应用于晶体坐标系的方法，通过引入“交叉张量”推广四元数乘积法则，使得四元数可以像在笛卡尔坐标系中一样用于晶体学中的旋转编码和组合，该乘积公式仅依赖于度量张量，并讨论了在电子背散射衍射（EBSD）中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单位四元数在科学中广泛用于编码旋转，但其通常形式基于笛卡尔基，无法直接用于晶体学（晶体基）。传统方法需要借助结构张量在晶体基与笛卡尔基之间来回变换坐标，效率低下。本文旨在消除这一坐标变换过程，使四元数能直接在晶体基中使用。

Method: 引入了一个称为“交叉张量”（cross tensor）的矩阵，该矩阵允许在晶体基中计算叉积，类似于度量张量允许在晶体基中计算标量积。研究发现交叉张量与度量张量的逆成正比。基于此，推导出了晶体学四元数乘积公式，该公式唯一地依赖于度量张量。

Result: 成功推导出了晶体学四元数的乘积法则，使其可直接在晶体基中使用，无需坐标变换。该乘积公式仅由晶体结构的度量张量决定。

Conclusion: 晶体学四元数的提出简化了四元数在晶体学中的应用，避免了笛卡尔基与晶体基之间的反复坐标变换。该方法对EBSD等晶体取向分析技术具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Unit quaternions are widely used in science to encode rotations because the quaternion product is more efficient than matrix product and more stable than Rodrigues product to calculate the composition of two rotations. However, quaternions in their usual form refers to a Cartesian basis; they cannot be used in crystallography as they are. The usual way to solve this issue to apply back-and-forth coordinate changes from the crystal basis to a Cartesian basis attached to the crystal with the help of the structure tensor. Here, we show that actually quaternions can be used directly in the crystal basis by generalizing the quaternion product law. In that aim, we introduced a matrix that we called cross tensor. It allows the calculation of the cross product in the crystal basis, a bit like the metric tensor allows it for the scalar product. We also show the cross tensor is proportional to the inverse of the metric tensor. The formula of crystallographic quaternion product is then given; it depends uniquely on the metric tensor. The application of the crystallographic quaternions to Electron Back Scatter Diffraction is discussed.

</details>


### [106] [Baldereschi mean value points for three-dimensional Bravais lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16974)
*N. D. Drummond*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文计算并列出了所有十四种三维Bravais晶格的Baldereschi平均值点，这些点是布里渊区中光滑周期函数接近其平均值的波矢，对量子蒙特卡洛等显式相关多电子方法尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Baldereschi点最初用于单电子方法，但作者意识到其在显式相关多电子方法（如量子蒙特卡洛模拟）中具有重要价值——这类方法在模拟超胞中只能使用单个Bloch波矢，因此需要精确的均值点来保证计算效率与准确性。

Method: 作者对所有十四种三维Bravais晶格进行了系统性的数学计算与数值求解，确定了每个晶格对应的Baldereschi平均值点。

Result: 成功计算并编制了全部十四种三维Bravais晶格的Baldereschi均值点表格。

Conclusion: Baldereschi均值点可有效推广至多电子显式相关方法，为量子蒙特卡洛等计算提供了重要的参考数据。

Abstract: The Baldereschi point of a crystal is a wavevector in the Brillouin zone at which every smooth periodic function of wavevector lies close to its mean value. Although originally introduced in the context of one-electron methods, mean-value points are ideal for explicitly correlated many-electron methods such as quantum Monte Carlo simulations, which can only use a single Bloch wavevector in the Brillouin zone of a simulation supercell. We have therefore evaluated and tabulated the Baldereschi mean-value points of all fourteen three-dimensional Bravais lattices.

</details>


### [107] [Giant Flat Band Amplification via Inertial Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17011)
*Wentao Mao,Stefano Gonella*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过惯性改造实现弹性材料中平带的策略，通过改变单元几何结构构建惯性锚点网络，使晶格分解为弱耦合谐振子阵列，从而在声子谱中产生平带。实验证实了该机制能实现高达两个数量级的放大、与源位置无关的多胞激活以及持续瞬态响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子材料中平带与紧致电子局域化相关，对超导、铁磁和强关联系统具有重要意义，但其在弹性介质中的物理意义尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过惯性改造（inertial retrofitting）改变经典晶格架构的单元几何结构，构建惯性锚点网络，将晶格分解为弱耦合的涌现谐振子阵列，使谐振在声子谱中表现为平带。

Result: 激光振动实验揭示了三个关键特征：相比通带和带隙条件放大高达两个数量级；与激励源位置无关的多胞激活；即使在多个激励周期后仍保持持久的瞬态响应。

Conclusion: 平带条件结合了局域化与扩展态属性，可诱导空间和时间上持久的巨大响应，为弹性波操控提供新范式。

Abstract: In electronic materials, flat bands are associated with compact electron localization, with implications for superconductivity, ferromagnetism and strongly correlated systems. The physical significance of their counterparts in elastic media is far less charted. Here we report a strategy to achieve elastic flat bands through an inertial retrofitting of classical lattice architectures. The idea is to alter the cell geometry to realize a network of inertial anchors, effectively partitioning the lattice into an array of weakly coupled emergent resonators, whose resonances appear as flat bands in the phonon spectrum. We demonstrate flat-band conditions that combine localized and extended state attributes and induce a giant response that is spatially and temporally persistent. Laser vibrometry experiments reveal three signatures of this mechanism: amplification up to two orders of magnitude compared to pass band and band gap conditions, multi-cell activation that is agnostic to the source location, and a persistent transient response even after several excitation cycles.

</details>


### [108] [STEP: Spin Tensor Equivariant Potential for Data-Efficient Learning of Magnetic Potential Energy Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17129)
*Yuanqing Gao,Wen-Hao Luo,Lei Zhang,Kun Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了自旋张量等变势（STEP），一种将矢量磁矩作为连续几何自由度嵌入等变表示的机器学习磁学原子间势，通过中心-环境张量积引入物理先验，在CrI₃、FeAl、CrN、Fe等体系上实现了高数据效率和高精度，能够准确描述声子、磁振子、磁振子-声子杂化以及居里温度等磁学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋极化第一性原理计算对不同非共线自旋-晶格构型计算代价高昂，亟需一种高效且精确的磁势能面建模方法。

Method: STEP将矢量磁矩作为连续几何自由度嵌入等变表示，通过中心-环境张量积（Center-Environment Tensor Product）耦合中心自旋表示与其局域自旋-晶格环境，支持特征层面的时间反演对称化，保持平移不变性和SO(3)等变性。

Result: 在CrI₃上展现了显著的数据效率；在FeAl、CrN、Fe基准上取得有竞争力或更优的精度；精确再现了CrI₃的声子色散和磁振子谱，捕捉到了各向异性磁相互作用；在Fe₂Mo₃O₈上定量描述了磁振子-声子杂化和磁振子极化子色散；STEP驱动的自旋动力学模拟得到的居里温度与实验吻合良好。

Conclusion: STEP是一种物理先验引导、数据高效、可扩展的框架，适用于自旋-晶格耦合、磁激发和有限温度磁性行为的建模。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient modeling of magnetic potential energy surfaces remains challenging because spin-polarized first-principles calculations for diverse non-collinear spin-lattice configurations are computationally demanding. Here we introduce the Spin Tensor Equivariant Potential (STEP), a magnetic machine-learning interatomic potential that treats vector magnetic moments as continuous geometric degrees of freedom and embeds them in an equivariant representation. By coupling the central spin representation to its local spin-lattice environment through a Center-Environment Tensor Product, STEP introduces a physics-informed bias while preserving translational invariance and $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ equivariance and supporting feature-level time-reversal symmetrization. Learning-curve analysis on monolayer CrI$_3$ shows that STEP achieves pronounced data efficiency, with higher-order tensor channels and iterative center-environment couplings leading to steep learning curves for energy, force, and magnetic force errors. On public FeAl, CrN, and Fe benchmarks, STEP achieves competitive or improved accuracy compared with recent magnetic machine-learning potentials. Using a compact but representative CrI$_3$ dataset, STEP reproduces phonon dispersions and magnon spectra with high fidelity, capturing subtle anisotropic magnetic interactions. For Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$, STEP further provides a quantitative description of magnon--phonon hybridization and reproduces its characteristic magnon polaron dispersion. Finally, spin dynamics simulations driven by STEP yield Curie temperatures for monolayer CrI$_3$ and bcc Fe in good agreement with experiments. These results establish STEP as a physically informed, data-efficient, and scalable framework for modeling spin-lattice coupling, magnetic excitations, and finite-temperature magnetic behavior.

</details>


### [109] [XMatcher: An Open-Source Framework for X-Ray Diffraction Phase Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17162)
*Bin Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: XMatcher是一个开源、基于证据驱动的晶体相鉴定框架，集成了衍射数据库、匹配算法和交互式可视化，支持多相识别和可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法虽加速了XRD衍射解读，但复杂样本仍需透明、基于证据的验证；而搜索匹配方法虽然是稳健的互补策略，但多数实现为专有软件，限制了可访问性和可重复性。

Method: XMatcher从晶体结构生成理论衍射库，通过化学和衍射约束检索候选相，应用全局角度偏移校正和一对一峰匹配，报告定量一致性指标和峰级证据；其AutoMix模块通过评估候选相组合、估计非负衍射贡献和可视化相特定峰分布来扩展到多相模式。

Result: XMatcher提供排序候选审查、交互式模式比较、PDF/CIF全模式验证和可重复分析导出；暴露支持和冲突证据而非依赖单一相似度分数，实现可解释和可重复的晶体相鉴定。

Conclusion: XMatcher作为一个开源、可解释、可重复的晶体相鉴定平台，通过整合多种证据而非单一相似度分数，为粉末XRD相位识别提供了更透明和可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (XRD) is widely used for crystalline phase identification, and recent machine learning approaches have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in accelerating diffraction interpretation. However, reliable phase assignment still requires transparent, evidence-based validation, particularly for complex samples where interpretability and expert assessment remain essential. Search-match methods provide a robust and complementary strategy, yet many implementations are proprietary, limiting accessibility and reproducibility. Here, we introduce XMatcher, an open-source, evidence-driven framework that integrates diffraction databases, matching algorithms, and interactive visualization into a portable workflow. XMatcher generates theoretical diffraction libraries from crystal structures, retrieves candidate phases through chemical and diffraction constraints, applies global angular-shift correction and one-to-one peak matching, and reports quantitative agreement metrics together with peak-level evidence. Its AutoMix module extends identification to multiphase patterns by evaluating candidate phase combinations, estimating non-negative diffraction contributions, and visualizing phase-specific peak distributions. Through a local graphical interface, XMatcher enables ranked candidate inspection, interactive pattern comparison, PDF/CIF-based whole-pattern validation, and reproducible analysis export. By exposing both supporting and conflicting evidence rather than relying on a single similarity score, XMatcher provides an interpretable and reproducible platform for crystalline phase identification.

</details>


### [110] [First-principles electron-phonon scattering in real-time TDDFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17265)
*Zhengwei Nie,Subhojit Pal,Marti Lüders,Alexander Buccheri,Hannes Hübener,Shunsuke A. Sato,Umberto De Giovannini*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发展了耗散实时含时密度泛函理论框架，通过引入基于电子-声子相互作用的碰撞积分，在保持量子相干实时传播的同时，实现了对激光驱动固体中载流子散射、弛豫和退相干过程的第一性原理描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统rt-TDDFT的幺正演化无法捕捉激光驱动固体中激发载流子向平衡态演化过程中不可逆的散射、弛豫和退相干过程，需要发展能够描述这些耗散过程的第一性原理方法。

Method: 在Born-Markov近似下，将第一性原理电子-声子相互作用通过自能导出的碰撞积分引入到约化单粒子密度矩阵的演化方程中，构建了耗散rt-TDDFT框架，在保持电子系统量子相干实时传播的同时引入声子介导的载流子能量和晶格动量重新分布。

Result: 发展了一个实用的第一性原理途径，能够模拟真实晶态材料中的弛豫、退相干以及时间分辨光谱信号。

Conclusion: 该耗散rt-TDDFT框架弥合了相干超快动力学与不可逆弛豫过程之间的鸿沟，为真实材料中非平衡载流子动力学的第一性原理模拟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-time time-dependent density functional theory provides a first-principles description of coherent electron dynamics in laser-driven solids, but its unitary formulation cannot capture the irreversible scattering, relaxation, and decoherence processes that drive excited carriers toward equilibrium. Here, we develop a dissipative rt-TDDFT framework in which first-principles electron-phonon interactions enter the evolution of the reduced one-body density matrix through self-energy-derived collision integrals within the Born-Markov approximation. The approach retains the quantum-coherent real-time propagation of the electronic system while introducing phonon-mediated transitions that redistribute carriers in energy and crystal momentum, thereby incorporating the microscopic momentum-transfer processes responsible for relaxation in real materials. The resulting framework provides a practical first-principles route to simulate relaxation, decoherence, and time-resolved spectroscopic signatures in realistic crystalline materials.

</details>


### [111] [Interfacial thermal transport in Si/SiC and SiC/diamond heterostructures: effects of amorphous interlayers and SiC polytypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17330)
*Pedram Mirchi,Shirin Sabokdast,Ali Rajabpour*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过非平衡分子动力学模拟，揭示了SiC多型体和非晶夹层对Si/SiC和SiC/金刚石界面声子热传输的影响。发现4H-SiC比3C-SiC具有更高的界面热导，而超薄非晶SiC层(0.5 nm)可在Si/3C-SiC体系中起到声子桥作用增强热传输，但更厚或富硅的非晶层会抑制热导。对于SiC/金刚石界面，任何非晶层都会严重降低热导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究SiC多型体(4H-SiC与3C-SiC)及不同厚度和成分的非晶夹层对Si/SiC和SiC/金刚石界面热传输的影响机制，为优化异质结界面热管理提供理论指导。

Method: 采用非平衡分子动力学模拟，计算界面热导(ITC)；结合振动态密度(VDOS)分析，评估声子谱匹配程度及非晶层对声子传输通道的影响。

Result: (1) 尖锐界面中，4H-SiC的ITC显著高于3C-SiC，因其具有更宽的活性声子谱和更优的声子谱匹配；(2) 0.5 nm超薄aSiC层使Si/3C-SiC的ITC从613升至716 MW/m²-K，呈现声子桥效应；(3) 更厚或富硅非晶层因增强非弹性声子散射而抑制ITC；(4) SiC/金刚石界面中任何非晶层(尤其aSi)都严重降低ITC。

Conclusion: 超薄非晶SiC层可在特定体系中作为声子桥增强界面热传输，但界面工程需要精确控制非晶层厚度和成分；对于高导热材料(如金刚石)，保持尖锐无缺陷界面至关重要。

Abstract: This study examines phonon-mediated heat transfer across Si/SiC and SiC/diamond interfaces using non-equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, emphasizing the influence of SiC polytypes and amorphous interlayers. For sharp interfaces, 4H-SiC exhibits considerably higher interfacial thermal conductance (ITC) than 3C-SiC, due to its broader active phonon spectrum and superior spectral matching with Si. While amorphous layers generally reduce ITC, a key observation is that an ultrathin 0.5-nm amorphous SiC (aSiC) layer can enhance heat transport in the Si/3C-SiC system: the ITC increases from 613 MW/m^2-K (sharp) to 716 MW/m^2-K, demonstrating a phonon-bridge effect. VDOS (vibrational density of states) analysis confirms that optimized ultrathin aSiC layers improve vibrational overlap and open additional phonon-transport channels. In contrast, thicker or silicon-rich amorphous layers significantly suppress ITC through enhanced inelastic phonon scattering. For SiC/diamond interfaces, any amorphous layer, particularly aSi, causes severe ITC degradation, highlighting the need for sharp, defect-free bonding to exploit diamond's high thermal conductivity.

</details>


### [112] [Response-function-optimized phase field modeling of solute trapping and solute drag in rapid alloy solidification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17370)
*Joni Kaipainen,Tatu Pinomaa,Nikolas Provatas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于优化的校准策略，通过将界面扩散率插值函数作为响应匹配自由度，将目标尖锐界面响应函数嵌入到稀合金相场模型中，实现了对快速凝固过程中速度依赖的界面性质（溶质截留、溶质拖曳、动力学液相线等）的精确预测。在Al-Cu合金中验证了该方法，并发现增加溶质拖曳使凝固形态从枝晶/细胞晶向混合枝晶-带状结构转变，最终演变为以带状生长为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速凝固微观结构的定量预测需要相场模型能准确表征界面性质的速度依赖性，但现有采用大界面以提高数值效率的相场模拟难以精确规定这些响应函数。因此需要一种能将目标尖锐界面响应函数嵌入相场模型的校准策略。

Method: 提出一种基于优化的校准方法：将界面扩散率插值函数作为响应匹配的自由度，通过一维稳态相场解获得优化的扩散率函数，并约束其再现预设连续生长模型目标的速度依赖性溶质截留和拖曳修正液相线动力学。扩展到稀多元合金体系。

Result: 在稀Al-Cu合金中，校准后的模型精确再现了中间溶质拖曳系数下的预设响应函数。二维定向凝固模拟表明，增加溶质拖曳使凝固形态从枝晶/细胞晶变为混合枝晶-带状结构，最终以带状生长为主。该方法成功扩展到稀多元合金。

Conclusion: 该框架为将实验、理论或原子模拟获得的非平衡界面动力学信息整合到定量相场模拟中提供了有效途径，可用于快速凝固合金的微观结构预测。

Abstract: Quantitative prediction of rapid solidification microstructures requires phase field models that represent the velocity dependence of interfacial properties, including solute partitioning, kinetic liquidus response, solute drag, and kinetic undercooling. These response functions control both microsegregation and morphology selection, but are difficult to prescribe accurately in phase field simulations that employ large interfaces for numerical efficiency. We introduce an optimization-based calibration strategy that embeds target sharp-interface response functions into a dilute alloy phase field formulation by treating the interfacial diffusivity interpolation function as a response-matching degree of freedom. The optimized diffusivity functions are obtained from one-dimensional steady-state phase field solutions, constrained to reproduce prescribed continuous-growth-model targets for velocity-dependent solute trapping and drag-modified liquidus kinetics. We demonstrate the calibrated model's accuracy and versatility in dilute Al-Cu by reproducing the prescribed response functions for intermediate solute drag coefficients relevant to rapid solidification. Two-dimensional directional-solidification simulations are conducted to isolate the effect of drag at fixed composition, thermal gradient, and pulling velocity. We show that increasing solute drag shifts the solidification morphology from dendritic/cellular growth to mixed dendritic-banded structures, and finally to predominantly banded growth. We extend the formulation to dilute multicomponent alloys, enabling independent specification of equilibrium partition coefficients and liquidus slopes for multiple solute species. The framework provides a route for incorporating experimentally, theoretically, or atomistically informed nonequilibrium interface kinetics into quantitative phase field simulations of rapidly solidified alloys.

</details>


### [113] [Symmetry-Engineered Nonlinear Hall Response and Optical Response in Strained Monolayer Janus AsTeBr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17385)
*Dimple Rani,Ritesh Ranjan Badhai,Gayatri Panda,Subrata Jana,Prasanjit Samal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算证明，单轴应变可打破Janus单层AsTeBr的C₃ᵥ对称性，诱导有限的贝里曲率偶极子，从而产生非线性霍尔效应，并在2%拉伸应变下达到最大响应；同时应变还会引起光学谱的红移和增强的低能带间跃迁，建立了几何相位、非线性霍尔输运与光学响应之间的微观联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有C₃ᵥ对称性的晶体中，三重旋转对称性迫使贝里曲率偶极子（BCD）为零，从而抑制了内禀非线性霍尔效应（NLHE），尽管系统中存在有限的局域贝里曲率。作者旨在探索如何通过应变工程打破这一对称性限制，在Janus二维材料中实现NLHE。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）结合基于Wannier函数的输运计算，研究单轴应变下Janus单层AsTeBr的电子结构、非线性霍尔效应及光学性质。

Result: 单轴应变将晶体对称性从C₃ᵥ降低至C₁，产生有限的BCD；应变诱导贝里曲率热点各向异性重新分布，在2%拉伸应变下获得最大非线性霍尔电导率和电流；光学谱呈现系统性红移，低能带间跃迁增强。

Conclusion: 研究建立了对称性破缺、贝里相位几何、非线性霍尔输运和光学响应之间的微观联系，证明单轴应变是调控Janus二维材料多功能特性的有效策略。

Abstract: The nonlinear Hall effect (NLHE) enables the generation of a transverse charge current in nonmagnetic materials with broken inversion symmetry while preserving time-reversal symmetry through the Berry curvature dipole (BCD). However, in crystals with $C_{3v}$ symmetry, the threefold rotational symmetry forces the BCD to vanish, thereby suppressing the intrinsic NLHE despite the presence of finite local Berry curvature. Here, using first-principles density functional theory combined with Wannier-based transport calculations, we demonstrate that uniaxial strain induces the NLHE in monolayer Janus AsTeBr by lowering the crystal symmetry from $C_{3v}$ to $C_{1}$ and generating a finite BCD. The resulting anisotropic redistribution of Berry-curvature hotspots produces pronounced nonlinear Hall conductivity and nonlinear Hall current, with the maximum response obtained at 2\% tensile strain. To elucidate the accompanying electronic-structure evolution, we further investigate the strain-dependent optical properties through the joint density of states, dielectric function, optical absorption, and reflectance. The optical spectra exhibit a systematic red shift and enhanced low-energy interband transitions, consistent with the strain-induced reconstruction of the electronic structure. Our results establish a microscopic connection between symmetry breaking, Berry-phase geometry, nonlinear Hall transport, and optical response, demonstrating that uniaxial strain provides an effective strategy for tailoring multiple functional properties in Janus two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [114] [Mechanical loss in amorphous solids: spatial correlations, interacting transitions, and annealed thermodynamic pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17434)
*Steven Blaber,Jörg Rottler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过分子模拟发现非晶固体中的机械耗散源于空间关联且强相互作用的跃迁网络，而非传统假设的独立两能级系统，并提出了基于退火和能量景观中主导弛豫路径分析来降低室温机械损耗的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非晶固体在低频机械振荡下的微观耗散机制，特别是用于改进地面引力波探测器镜面镀层的机械损耗性能。

Method: 采用分子模拟方法，结合退火研究和能量景观中主导弛豫路径的分析。

Result: 发现跃迁具有空间关联性和强相互作用，不能用独立两能级系统的叠加来描述，必须采用一般网络描述。

Conclusion: 提出了降低室温机械损耗的新机制，为改进非晶固体材料（尤其是引力波探测器镜面镀层）的机械性能提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The disordered and defect-rich structure of amorphous solids forms heterogeneous, high-dimensional energy landscapes. Such an energy landscape can be described by a discrete-state network of transitions between stable energy minima. Under low-frequency mechanical oscillations, defect-mediated, thermally activated transitions provide a microscopic mechanism for mechanical dissipation that are the dominant cause of mechanical loss in the mirror coatings of ground based gravitational waves detectors. Using molecular simulations, we find spatially correlated and strongly interacting transitions that require a general network description instead of a superposition of independent two-level systems as traditionally assumed. An annealing study combined with an analysis of dominant relaxation paths in the energy landscape reveals novel mechanisms for reducing room temperature mechanical loss.

</details>


### [115] [Going Beyond the d-band Center to Design Intermetallic Catalysts for Nitrogen Reduction: A High-Throughput DFT and Machine Learning Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17463)
*Parastoo Agharezaei,Kulbir Kaur Ghuman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究结合高通量DFT计算与机器学习，构建了47种双金属间化合物的约1200个数据点，通过仅20个关键特征实现了对NRR中间体吸附能的准确预测（MAE 0.17-0.39 eV）。SHAP分析揭示s-和p-带特征对吸附强度的贡献超过传统d-带中心，挑战了d-带中心范式，重新定义了金属间化合物NRR催化剂的电子描述符空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氮还原反应（NRR）是可持续氨合成的关键，但传统催化剂设计过度依赖d-带中心理论，忽视了s-和p-轨道在金属间化合物（IMCs）中的潜在贡献，亟需建立更全面的电子描述符体系来指导非贵金属催化剂的高效筛选。

Method: 构建47种双金属IMCs数据集，系统评估约1200个表面位点上的N₂、N₂H和NH₃吸附能；提取s-、p-、d-带中心和填充度、Bader电荷等电子描述符；利用仅20个关键特征训练简单高效的ML模型，并通过SHAP分析进行特征重要性解释。

Result: ML模型对N₂、N₂H和NH₃吸附能的平均绝对误差分别为0.26 eV、0.39 eV和0.17 eV；SHAP分析表明p-和s-带特征对吸附强度贡献显著大于d-带中心，尤其在N₂和N₂H中间体上；s-和p-轨道对Fe-Co等过渡金属合金活性也有重要贡献，源于吸附诱导的sp-d杂化。

Conclusion: 本研究挑战了以d-带为中心的传统范式，重新定义了金属间化合物NRR催化剂的电子描述符空间，将s-和p-带描述符确立为关键但被忽视的贡献因素，为DFT-ML指导的非贵金属可持续氨合成催化剂发现奠定了基础。

Abstract: This study combines high-throughput DFT calculations with machine learning techniques to uncover the key descriptors governing the nitrogen reduction reaction (NRR) in intermetallic compounds (IMCs). A dataset of 47 bimetallic IMCs was constructed, and the adsorption energies of key intermediates (N2, N2H, and NH3) were systematically evaluated across all accessible surface sites, yielding approximately 1,200 data points. By incorporating intrinsic material properties along with electronic descriptors including s-, p- and d-band centers and fillings, as well as Bader charges of atoms neighboring the adsorbate, predictive ML models were developed with mean absolute errors of 0.26 eV for N2, 0.39 eV for N2H, and 0.17 eV for NH3 adsorption. Importantly, accurate predictions are obtained with only 20 key features, enabling the use of simple and computationally efficient ML models. SHAP analysis indicates that p- and s-band characteristics play a more prominent role in determining adsorption strength than the traditionally used d-band center, particularly for N2 and N2H intermediates. Beyond their established importance in systems containing p-block elements or nearly filled d-band metals, s- and p-orbitals are also found to contribute significantly to transition-metal alloys activity such as Fe-Co, driven by adsorption-induced sp-d hybridization. By challenging the d-band-centric paradigm and identifying s- and p-band descriptors as critical yet overlooked contributors, this work redefines the electronic descriptor space for intermetallic NRR catalysts and lays the groundwork for DFT-ML-guided discovery of non-noble materials for sustainable ammonia synthesis.

</details>


### [116] [Non-equilibrium Effects in Vibrational Modes Pumped by Inelastic Tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17530)
*Chi Ming Yim,Owain T. Beynon,Seunghyun Khim,Chiara Gattinoni,Peter Wahl*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用扫描隧道显微镜中的非弹性电子隧穿技术，在强关联电子材料PdCrO2的Pd终端表面上驱动局域振动模式偏离平衡态，发现氢吸附形成的氢簇中的振动激发具有比典型金属表面更长的寿命，并通过理论计算将这种长寿命特性归因于衬底的独特性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联电子材料的性质对小晶格畸变异常敏感，可通过选择性畸变驱动（通常借助光学激发）进行调控。本文旨在探索利用非平衡振动激发作为调控手段的可行性。

Method: 利用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）中的非弹性电子隧穿技术，在PdCrO2的Pd终端表面驱动振动激发，通过研究氢吸附形成的不同尺寸和形状的氢簇中局域模式的动力学行为来分析振动激发特性。

Result: 氢簇中的振动激发比典型金属表面具有更长的寿命；光谱数据呈现非平衡效应的特征，归因于这些模式的延长寿命；理论计算支持长寿命激发与衬底独特性质相关。

Conclusion: 在强关联电子材料表面可通过非弹性电子隧穿实现非平衡振动激发，且由于衬底的特殊性质，这些振动模式具有长寿命特征，为材料调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: The properties of strongly correlated electron materials exhibit a surprising sensitivity to small lattice distortions, providing an opportunity for their tuning by selective distortion driving, usually achieved by optical excitations. Using inelastic electron tunneling in scanning tunneling microscopy, we demonstrate that at the surface of a strongly correlated electron material, we can drive vibrational excitations out of equilibrium, by studying the dynamics of localized modes on the Pd-terminated surface of the delafossite oxide PdCrO2. This surface forms a tiling of hydrogen clusters of varying sizes and shapes upon hydrogen adsorption. Our findings reveal that vibrational excitations in the clusters exhibit longer lifetimes than on typical metal surfaces. Detailed analysis of the spectroscopy data reveals signatures of non-equilibrium effects in the excitations which we attribute to the extended lifetimes of these modes. Theoretical calculations support that the long-lived nature of the excitations is related to the unique properties of the substrate.

</details>


### [117] [CuCrZr heat-sink irradiation performance reveals new challenges for thermonuclear fusion reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17594)
*Thomas Barzic,Anna-Carina Seitlinger,Christoph Frühwirth,Edward McDonald,Jing Tang,Jonathan A. Hinks,Alexandr Zinovev,Dmitry Terentyev,Stefan Luidold,Cláudio G. Schön,Enrique Jimenez-Melero,Stefan Pogatscher,Matheus A. Tunes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发现聚变反应堆中CuCrZr合金的时效强化纳米析出相在辐照下会因弹道溶解和热溶解再析出两种机制而退化，同时He泡和Kr空穴成核以及五年嬗变不可逆地将合金化学转向Ni-Zr金属间化合物，这三种独立机制共同挑战了CuCrZr合金作为散热材料的长期性能，揭示了需要开发新型铜基散热合金以应对聚变中子谱带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业聚变能需要能够承受强烈中子轰击并提取极端热负荷的材料，CuCrZr合金作为聚变反应堆领先的散热材料，其强度来源于时效热处理形成的纳米析出相分散体，但这种析出强化策略能否承受聚变相关辐照尚未得到测试。

Method: 结合原位透射电子显微镜（重离子辐照和He注入）、热力学建模和嬗变建模。

Result: 硬化析出相在两种对立动力学机制下溶解：低温以弹道溶解为主，高温以溶解和再析出为主。He泡和Kr-rich空穴在空位可移动后成核，五年服务期的嬗变不可逆地将合金化学转向Ni-Zr金属间化合物。

Conclusion: 三种独立机制共同表明CuCrZr合金的时效强化微结构在长期中子暴露下难以保持完整，长期性能需要进一步评估；聚变反应堆设计和商业化面临新的材料挑战，即需要开发能够在化学成分被聚变中子谱不可逆改写的情况下仍能保持工程强度的新型铜基散热合金。

Abstract: Commercial fusion energy requires materials that survive intense neutron bombardment whilst extracting extreme heat loads for conversion to electricity. The CuCrZr alloy, the leading heat-sink material for fusion reactors, derives its strength from a fine dispersion of nano-precipitates formed during prime-ageing heat-treatment. Whether this precipitation-hardening strategy can withstand fusion-relevant irradiation remains untested. Here we show, combining in situ transmission electron microscopy under heavy-ion irradiation and He implantation with thermodynamic and transmutation modelling, that the hardening precipitates dissolve under two opposing kinetic regimes: ballistic dissolution dominates at low temperatures, whilst dissolution and re-precipitation dominate at high temperatures. Although the accelerated dose rates inherent to ion irradiation shift the balance between ballistic mixing and thermal back-diffusion relative to reactor conditions, precipitate degradation at both kinetic extremes indicates that the prime-aged microstructure is unlikely to remain unaltered under prolonged neutron exposure. He bubbles and Kr-rich voids nucleate once vacancies become mobile, and transmutation over five service years irreversibly redirects the alloy chemistry towards Ni-Zr intermetallics. These three independent mechanisms converge to challenge the strategy on which CuCrZr performance depends, suggesting that the long-term performance of age-hardenable Cu-based heat-sink alloys in fusion reactors warrants further assessment. Our findings reveal a new materials challenge for fusion reactor design and commercialisation: the need for new Cu-based heat-sink alloys able to retain engineered strength whilst their chemistry is irreversibly rewritten - thermodynamically and ballistically - by the fusion neutron spectrum.

</details>


### [118] [d-band filling dictates magnetic stability in Mn- and Co-substituted FeRh alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17688)
*Greeshma R,Rudra Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，系统研究了B2有序Fe基合金中Mn和Co替代Fe位点对磁性的影响。发现Mn掺杂（空穴掺杂）导致自旋极化崩溃和反铁磁竞争，引发“巡游磁软性”使居里温度下降约450 K；而Co掺杂（电子掺杂）将费米能级钉扎在多数自旋赝能隙中，稳定铁磁交换，起到“磁硬化”作用。研究建立了d带填充作为巡游磁体磁稳定性的主控参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究B2有序Fe基合金中组分依赖的磁性行为，揭示d带填充对巡游磁体磁稳定性的调控机制，区别于传统的磁体积效应模型。

Method: 采用基于相干势近似（CPA）的第一性原理计算，在Fe亚晶格上系统替代Mn和Co，研究组分依赖的磁性能。

Result: Mn掺杂导致自旋极化在x≈0.5处过零，出现反铁磁竞争（η_Mn<0），但铁磁态与AFM-II态能量差仍较大（ΔE~0.35 eV/atom）；Co掺杂维持高自旋极化（|P|≈0.75），稳定铁磁交换。

Conclusion: 通过调控费米能级相对于赝能隙的位置（即d带填充）可系统控制B2有序巡游磁体的磁稳定性，提供了一种区别于传统磁体积效应的微观调控框架。

Abstract: The composition-dependent magnetic properties of B2-ordered \zfr~alloys with substitutional disorder on the Fe sublattice are
  investigated using first-principles calculations within the coherent potential approximation. By systematically substituting Mn
  and Co on the Fe sublattice, we establish $d$-band filling as the primary control parameter governing magnetic stability in this
  itinerant system. Mn substitution (hole doping) shifts the Fermi level into the minority-spin bonding states, driving a collapse
  of spin polarization (crossing zero at $x \approx 0.5$) and the emergence of competing antiferromagnetic interactions
  ($η_\mathrm{Mn} < 0$). Even though the ferromagnetic configuration remains energetically well separated from the G-type
  AFM-II configuration across the studied range ($ΔE$ up to $\sim$0.35~eV/atom), this exchange competition drives an
  ``itinerant magnetic softness'' that suppresses the Curie temperature by $\sim$450~K -- a finite-temperature instability set by
  the near-cancellation of competing exchange interactions rather than by AFM--FM energy proximity. In contrast, Co substitution
  (electron doping) acts as a ``magnetic hardener'' by pinning the Fermi level within the majority-spin pseudogap, preserving high
  spin polarization ($|P| \approx 0.75$) and stabilizing ferromagnetic exchange across the full composition range. These results
  show that tuning the Fermi level relative to the pseudogap provides a systematic, microscopic framework for controlling magnetic
  stability in B2-ordered itinerant magnets, distinct from simple magneto-volume models.

</details>


### [119] [Efficient Quantum-Mechanical Modeling of Nonradiative Charge Transfer Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17730)
*Alexander Karl,Dominic Waldhoer,Theresia Knobloch,Christoph Wilhelmer,Tibor Grasser*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统地发展了基于非辐射多声子（NMP）理论的电荷俘获与发射速率计算方法，提出了具有交叉保持近似特性的全解析、数值稳定且计算高效的跃迁率表达式，并推导了局域态与扩展能带之间跃迁的连续体公式，从而在保持量子力学物理本质的前提下实现了大规模半导体器件模拟中电荷转移过程的实用化计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非辐射电荷转移过程在半导体器件可靠性现象（如偏置温度不稳定性、迟滞、随机电报噪声、陷阱辅助隧穿）中至关重要，但全量子力学的NMP公式计算成本过高，无法应用于大规模模拟。因此，需要开发在保持物理严谨性的同时具备计算可行性的近似方法。

Method: 从量子力学基础的电子-声子耦合动力学出发，推导出计算效率高的电荷俘获与发射速率近似表达式；引入交叉保持近似（crossing-preserving approximation），获得全解析、数值稳定且计算成本低的跃迁率公式；并进一步推导局域缺陷态与扩展电子能带之间跃迁的连续体公式。

Result: 提出了一种交叉保持近似方法，所得跃迁率表达式具有全解析形式、数值稳定性好、计算成本低，同时保留了量子力学物理本质；建立了局域态与扩展能带之间跃迁的连续体描述，可直接集成到半导体器件模拟中。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了连接微观缺陷物理与大规模半导体器件模拟的桥梁，为在实际大规模模拟中实现基于NMP的物理模型提供了系统的理论推导、实现指导以及明确的适用性边界说明。

Abstract: Nonradiative charge transfer processes play a central role in a wide range of physical phenomena, including reliability phenomena in semiconductor devices such as bias temperature instability, hysteresis, random telegraph noise, and trap-assisted tunneling. nonradiative multiphonon (NMP) theory provides a physically rigorous framework for describing such charge transitions, but its full quantum-mechanical formulation is computationally too demanding for large-scale simulations. In this work, we present a systematic and implementation-oriented treatment of NMP-based models for practical large-scale simulations. Starting from the quantum-mechanical foundations of coupled electron--phonon dynamics, we derive computationally efficient approximations for charge capture and emission rates and clearly identify the underlying assumptions and validity regimes. In particular, we introduce an effective crossing-preserving approximation that yields fully analytic, numerically stable, and computationally inexpensive transition rates while retaining the essential quantum-mechanical physics. The resulting expressions are therefore well suited for large-scale device simulations, where capture coefficients must be evaluated repeatedly over broad multidimensional parameter spaces. Furthermore, we derive continuum formulations for transitions between localized defect states and extended electronic bands, enabling direct incorporation into semiconductor-device simulations. The resulting framework bridges microscopic defect physics and practical large-scale simulations of charge transfer processes in complex semiconductor devices. At the same time this work serves as a practical guide for implementing physically grounded NMP-based models, providing both a systematic derivation of the underlying theory and a clear guidance on the validity limits.

</details>


### [120] [Physically motivated iso-orbital indicator for meta-GGA exchange functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17736)
*Jeet Sharma,Abhishek Bhattacharjee,Bikash Patra,Prasanjit Samal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对meta-GGA泛函中iso-orbital指示符α在低密度尾区发散的问题，提出用物理驱动的Pauli动能密度替代原分母，无需经验正则化参数即可消除发散。在r²SCAN和MS2交换泛函中测试LKT和PGS两种增强因子，发现改进后的指示符抑制了半局域交换势的非物理振荡，恢复了原子尾区的正确电子局域化。对十种立方半导体的带隙平均绝对误差降低41.1%（r²SCAN@PGS）和48.8%（MS2@PGS），同时保持内聚能精度。两种不同构型泛函的一致性改进证实了该方案的物理起源而非泛函特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: meta-GGA泛函中的iso-orbital指示符α在低密度尾区发散，导致非物理的交换势和系统性的带隙误差，且现有方法依赖经验正则化参数。

Method: 将α的分母替换为来自无轨道DFT理论的物理Pauli动能密度（LKT和PGS两种增强因子），在r²SCAN和MS2交换泛函中测试。

Result: 抑制了半局域交换势的非物理振荡，恢复了原子尾区电子局域化；带隙MAE降低41.1%（r²SCAN@PGS）和48.8%（MS2@PGS），内聚能精度基本保持。

Conclusion: 该改进具有物理而非泛函特异性的起源，为开发满足约束条件的iso-orbital指示符的meta-GGA泛函提供了动力。

Abstract: The iso-orbital indicator $α= (τ- τ^\mathrm{vW})/τ^\mathrm{UEG}$ is a key ingredient of meta-generalized gradient approximation (meta-GGA) functionals, but diverges in low-density tails , causing unphysical exchange potentials and systematic band gap errors as noted in [J. Chem. Phys. 150, 161101 (2019)]. We replace the denominator of $α$ with a physically motivated Pauli KED drawn from the orbital-free DFT literature, eliminating the divergence in the low density atomic tail without any empirical regularization parameter. Testing two such enhancement factors: LKT and PGS, within the r$^2$SCAN and MS2 exchange functionals, we find that the modified indicators suppress spurious oscillations in the semilocal exchange potential and restore correct electron localization in atomic tails. For a ten-member cubic semiconductor benchmark, the band gap mean absolute error is reduced by 41.1 % for r$^2$SCAN@PGS and 48.8 % for MS2@PGS, while cohesive energy accuracy is largely preserved. The consistent improvement across two functionals with distinct constructions confirms a physical rather than functional specific origin, and motivates further development of meta-GGA functionals with constraint satisfying iso-orbital indicators.

</details>


### [121] [Localized crystallization of Ce:YIG thin films on Si using CO2 laser annealing for integrated nonreciprocal photonic device applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17905)
*Xinran Ji,Junxian Wang,Tianchi Zhang,Xuan Zhao,Di Wu,Zixuan Wei,Yizhi Chen,Jialong Wang,Lei Bi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了激光退火技术在铈替代钇铁石榴石（Ce:YIG）薄膜结晶化中的应用，成功在SiO2衬底和氮化硅（SiN）波导上实现了石榴石相，制备了MZI型光学隔离器，在1552.7 nm波长下实现了27.1 dB的隔离比和10.1 dB的插入损耗，饱和法拉第旋转角为-2317.7 deg/cm，传播损耗为188.2 dB/cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光退火技术已被证明是磁光石榴石薄膜在半导体衬底上局域结晶化的有效方法，但此前尚无研究探讨利用激光退火技术制备铈替代钇铁石榴石（Ce:YIG）薄膜并将其用于集成光子器件。

Method: 在SiO2衬底和氮化硅（SiN）光子波导上，对溅射沉积的Ce:YIG薄膜（采用激光退火的Y3Fe5O12籽晶层）进行激光退火处理，随后制备MZI型光学隔离器并测试其性能。

Result: 成功在SiO2衬底和SiN波导上观察到石榴石相；Ce:YIG薄膜的磁光性能与快速热退火（RTA）制备的薄膜相当；制备的MZI隔离器饱和法拉第旋转角-2317.7 deg/cm，传播损耗188.2 dB/cm，在1552.7 nm波长下隔离比27.1 dB，插入损耗10.1 dB。

Conclusion: 激光退火技术可有效实现Ce:YIG薄膜的局域结晶化，其性能可与RTA工艺媲美，为集成非互易光子器件提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Laser annealing (LA) technique has emerged as an effective method for localized crystallization of magneto-optical (MO) garnet thin films on semiconductor substrates. However, no studies have explored the crystallization and magneto-optical (MO) properties of cerium-substituted yttrium iron garnet (Ce:YIG, Ce1Y2Fe5O12) thin films for integrated photonic device applications using LA technique. In this study, we provide a comprehensive investigation into the laser annealing of Ce:YIG films deposited on SiO2 substrates and silicon nitride photonic waveguides for integrated nonreciprocal photonic device applications. Garnet phase was successfully observed in films grown on SiO2 substrates, and SiN waveguides with laser annealing of sputtered Ce:YIG films on top of a laser annealed Y3Fe5O12 seed layer. The magneto-optical (MO) properties of Ce:YIG films on oxidized Si substrates were found to be comparable to those prepared by rapid thermal annealing (RTA). A Mach-Zehnder Interferometer (MZI) type optical isolator based on Ce:YIG film on SiN was fabricated, exhibiting a saturation Faraday rotation of -2317.7 deg/cm and propagation loss of 188.2 dB/cm. Isolation ratio of 27.1 dB and insertion loss of 10.1 dB were achieved at 1552.7 nm wavelength.

</details>


### [122] [Chemical filters for ultra-high-throughput materials screening and generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17910)
*Kinga O. Mastej,Panyalak Detrattanawichai,Hyunsoo Park,Anthony Onwuli,Masahiro Negishi,Aron Walsh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于化合价规则的化学有效性算子（Chemical Validity Operator），通过SMACT包的数据驱动氧化态模型，将启发式化学规则转化为可配置算法先验，用于评估和引导生成式材料发现，并通过过滤、奖励函数等方式提升生成模型输出的化学合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在材料设计中生成的大量成分违反已知化学原理，限制了生成模型的可靠性和可解释性，因此需要一种能够编码化学启发式规则的方法来约束和评估生成结果。

Method: 基于开源SMACT包构建数据驱动的氧化态模型，通过可调阈值实现从宽松到保守的化学约束连续插值；将该算子作为筛选工具和强化学习奖励函数，引导潜扩散模型生成化学合理的组成。

Result: 对六种最先进的无机晶体生成模型进行基准测试，发现大多数模型虽能再现化学计量比，但低估了真实氧化态组合；过滤操作可移除依赖罕见氧化态的成分，同时保留凸包附近的低能化合物。

Conclusion: 通过编码化学启发式规则和经验观察，为氧化态感知的生成模型奠定基础，提升了生成式材料设计的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence is rapidly transforming materials design by enabling de novo exploration of immense chemical spaces. Yet a large proportion of AI-generated compositions remain implausible, violating established chemical principles, which limits the reliability and interpretability of generative materials design. Here, we introduce a chemical validity operator that recasts heuristic chemical rules as a configurable algorithmic prior for evaluating and guiding generative materials discovery. Built on the open-source SMACT package, a data-informed oxidation-state model exposes tunable thresholds, allowing users to interpolate continuously between permissive and conservative chemical constraints, while supporting both exploratory and conservative materials-design workflows. Benchmarking six state-of-the-art generative models for inorganic crystals shows that most reproduce stoichiometry but under-represent realistic oxidation-state combinations, and that filtering removes compositions reliant on rarely observed oxidation states while preserving low-energy compounds near the convex hull. Beyond screening, the same operator can also serve as a reinforcement-learning reward, steering a latent diffusion model towards chemically grounded compositions. By encoding chemical heuristics and observations, this work establishes a foundation for oxidation-state-aware generative models.

</details>


### [123] [The Recurrent Structural Frameworks of Stable Inorganic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17911)
*Roee Asher,Nadav Moav,Lee A. Burton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过对23,160种热力学凸包上的无机材料结构进行分析，发现稳定无机材料集中在有限的6,820种结构框架中，其中2,382种为跨化学体系重复出现的结构原型。金属体系的结构重复性尤为突出，6,270种材料归入535种框架和327种原型，而5,557种离子化合物则分布在1,704种框架和696种原型中。研究建立了一个基于重复性的结构原型目录，可用于评估未来材料中结构框架的相对出现概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可存在的晶体结构数量远超已实现的热力学稳定无机材料数量，表明实验可触及的结构空间围绕有限的一组优选结构框架组织，揭示稳定材料在结构空间中的分布规律。

Method: 对23,160种位于热力学凸包上的结构进行分析，识别出6,820种不同结构框架，将跨多种化学体系重复出现的2,382种框架归类为结构原型，并对金属体系和离子化合物体系分别进行统计。

Result: 金属体系中6,270种材料归入535种框架和327种原型；离子化合物中5,557种材料分布在1,704种框架和696种原型中。

Conclusion: 稳定无机材料占据高度可压缩的结构空间区域，基于重复性建立的结构原型目录为评估框架相对出现概率提供了经验依据。

Abstract: The number of possible crystal structures vastly exceeds the number realized among thermodynamically stable inorganic materials, suggesting that experimentally accessible structure space is organized around a limited set of preferred structural frameworks. Analysis of 23,160 structures on the thermodynamic convex hull identifies 6,820 distinct structural frameworks, of which 2,382 recur across multiple chemically distinct materials and are therefore classified as structural prototypes. Within a filtered dataset, metallic systems exhibit particularly strong structural recurrence, with 6,270 materials grouped into 535 frameworks and 327 prototypes, while 5,557 ionic compounds occupy 1,704 frameworks and 696 prototypes. These results demonstrate that stable inorganic materials occupy a highly compressible region of structure space and establish a recurrence-based catalog of structural prototypes that provides an empirical measure of framework prevalence, enabling the relative likelihood of structural frameworks in future materials to be estimated from their recurrence among known compounds.

</details>


### [124] [Informatics Modeling of High Tg Polymers: Assessing the Role of Processing versus Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17925)
*Qinrui Liu,Scott R. Broderick*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于聚合物拓扑描述符的机器学习模型预测玻璃化转变温度（Tg），验证了Tg主要由化学结构和分子拓扑决定，但发现溶液浇铸和高温退火制备的聚合物因强分子间相互作用和加工条件影响而出现预测偏差，表明该方法可筛分出加工条件敏感的聚合物类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于结构的聚合物性质建模取得了进展，但受分子间相互作用和加工条件强烈影响的聚合物的Tg预测仍然具有挑战性。先前开发的基于拓扑描述符的机器学习模型仅依赖化学结构，未包含加工参数，本文旨在扩展该模型的适用范围并探究加工参数对Tg预测的影响。

Method: 将先前基于聚合物拓扑描述符的机器学习模型应用于更广泛的聚合物数据集，并将加工参数整合到特征集中，对比纯化学结构模型与包含加工参数模型的预测性能，分析预测偏差与聚合物类型及加工条件的关系。

Result: 化学结构模型对大多数聚合物表现出一致的预测性能；但部分聚合物（尤其是通过溶液浇铸和高温退火制备的）预测值与实验值存在偏差，这些偏差与强分子间相互作用和加工依赖性因素相关。

Conclusion: 分子拓扑为Tg预测提供了坚实基础，但该方法也能筛选出那些加工条件起重要作用的聚合物类别，揭示了化学/结构主导与加工条件主导的边界。

Abstract: Despite the advances in structure-based modeling of polymer properties, accurately predicting glass transition temperature (Tg) is still challenging for polymers whose behavior is strongly influenced by intermolecular interactions and processing conditions. We previously developed a machine-learning model based on polymer topological descriptors to predict Tg. The model performed well and was based solely on the chemistry and structure of the polymer without any inclusion of processing parameters. In this work, we have extended that work by first applying that same model to a larger range of polymers and second by integrating processing parameters into the feature set. The chemistry-based model still demonstrates consistent predictive performance for most polymers, indicating that Tg is indeed primarily chemistry and structure driven and not strongly impacted by processing. However, several polymers exhibited deviations between predicted and experimental Tg values. Detailed analysis reveals that these differences are related to strong intermolecular interactions and processing-dependent factors, particularly for polymers prepared by solution casting and high temperature annealing. These results demonstrate that molecular topology provides a strong foundation for Tg prediction; however, this approach also screens out those classes of polymers for with processing conditions play an important role.

</details>


### [125] [Spin-phonon interaction in a symmetry-enforced spin-polarized state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17980)
*Suman Kalyan Pradhan,Dayal Das,Shubham Patel,Subhajit Mahapatra,Sachin Majee,Dibyendu Majee,Arnab Bera,Achintya Singha,Snehasish Nandy,Samik DuttaGupta,Atindra Nath Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过拉曼光谱和第一性原理计算，在g型交变磁体CoNb4Se8中发现了由自旋-轨道耦合介导的对称性选择性声子重整化现象，揭示了对称性驱动磁材料中一种不同于传统交换-伸缩机制的自旋-声子耦合新机制，表明声子可作为探测对称性诱导自旋极化的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性驱动磁材料（如交变磁体）缺乏净磁化但具有自旋极化电子态，然而晶格动力学如何与对称性衍生的自旋极化电子态耦合尚不清楚，自旋-声子耦合机制缺乏理解，限制了这类材料的自旋调控应用。

Method: 采用温度分辨和偏振分辨拉曼光谱测量g型交变磁体CoNb4Se8及其缺Co结构类似物，结合第一性原理计算，分析声子模式随磁相变的重整化行为及对称性选择性。

Result: 在CoNb4Se8中观测到跨磁相变的对称性选择性声子重整化，在缺乏长程磁有序的缺Co体系中也存在类似声子反常；第一性原理计算表明自旋-轨道耦合建立了晶格振动与对称性控制电子态之间的对称性依赖的相互作用通道。

Conclusion: 发现了对称性驱动磁材料中不同于传统交换-伸缩机制的自旋-声子耦合新机制，声子可作为对称性驱动自旋极化的灵敏探针，为理解和设计对称性驱动量子材料中的自旋-晶格功能提供了框架。

Abstract: Symmetry-governed magnetic materials have emerged as a promising platform for spintronic functionalities without net magnetization or stray magnetic fields, motivating the exploration of how lattice dynamics couple to symmetry-derived spin-polarized electronic states. Understanding spin-phonon coupling in these systems is therefore essential for uncovering the microscopic origin of spin-lattice interactions and for enabling their control in quantum materials. However, this mechanism remains poorly understood because spin polarization originates from crystal symmetry rather than conventional magnetic order. Here, we address this issue in the g-type altermagnet CoNb4Se8 using temperature- and polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy, complemented by measurements on a structurally analogous Co-deficient compound lacking well-defined long-range magnetic order. We observe pronounced symmetry-selective phonon renormalization across the magnetic transition in CoNb4Se8, while related phonon anomalies persist in the Co-deficient system, demonstrating that the lattice response cannot be explained solely by conventional exchange-striction associated with coherent magnetic ordering. First-principles calculations reveal that spin-orbit coupling establishes a symmetry-dependent interaction channel between lattice vibrations and symmetry-governed electronic states. Our results identify an alternative mechanism for spin-phonon coupling in symmetry-governed magnetic materials and demonstrate that phonons provide a sensitive probe of symmetry-driven spin polarization even without robust magnetic order. More broadly, this work provides a framework for understanding and engineering spin-lattice functionality in symmetry-driven quantum materials, offering design principles for coupling lattice dynamics to spin-polarized electronic states.

</details>


### [126] [Foundry CMOS platform for multimodal quantum materials characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18059)
*Sharad Kumar Yadav,Luca Nessi,Ondrej Dyck,Jinchen Wang,Bogdan Dryzhakov,Alex Melendez,Huan Zhao,Qian Song,Doha Amer,Cole Brabec,Saleh Alqazlan,Ruonan Han,Riccardo Comin,Stephen Jesse,Dirk Englund,Jawaher Almutlaq*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种利用商用65纳米CMOS工艺构建的无源、可代工厂制造的多模态量子材料表征平台，在1 mm²面积内集成了微波、热学和电子子系统，实现了低温磁化率测量、NV中心ODMR（对比度>20%，灵敏度2-3 µT/√Hz）以及原位电子束成像兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子材料实验依赖定制化硬件，导致可重复性和可扩展性受限，亟需一种标准化、可规模化制造的多模态表征平台。

Method: 利用商用65纳米CMOS工艺的金属堆叠层，通过功能分区在同一芯片内集成射频、热学和电子子系统，形成被动式表征平台。

Result: 在1.75 K下实现了Fe₃GeTe₂异质结构的低温磁化率测量；NV-ODMR对比度>20%，微波功率4-9 dBm，比传统天线方案降低20-25 dB，灵敏度2-3 µT/√Hz；兼容原位电子束成像且无图像质量退化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可代工厂制造、可扩展的多模态量子传感与材料表征平台，有望推动量子材料研究的标准化和产业化。

Abstract: Quantum materials experiments increasingly rely on microwave, electrical, thermal, optical, and structural probes, but these capabilities are typically assembled from custom hardware that limits reproducibility and scalability. Here we show that a commercial 65-nm CMOS process can be repurposed as a passive, foundry-manufacturable characterization platform by functionally partitioning its metal stack into microwave, thermal, and electrical subsystems within a 1 mm2 footprint. The integrated RF architecture enables cryogenic magnetic susceptibility measurements of Fe3GeTe2 heterostructures at 1.75 K without sample-specific fabrication. We further demonstrate NV-center optically detected magnetic resonance (ODMR) with >20% contrast at 4-9 dBm microwave power, reducing power requirements by 20-25 dB relative to conventional antenna-based approaches while maintaining sensitivities of 2-3 uT/sqrt(Hz). We additionally confirm compatibility with in-situ electron-beam imaging, showing no measurable degradation in image quality upon device operation. These results establish a scalable, foundry-manufacturable platform for multimodal quantum sensing and materials characterization.

</details>


### [127] [First-principles electronic transport properties of Ti and Ti-6Al-4V for modeling ultrashort-pulse laser ablation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18085)
*Korbinian Hobmaier,Guillaume E. Allemand,Alberto Marmodoro,Matthieu J. Verstraete,Ján Minár,Heinz P. Huber,David Redka*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文从头计算了钛合金Ti-6Al-4V和纯钛在超短脉冲激光烧蚀中的电子输运参数，发现合金的电子热导率仅为纯钛的1/6.4，但使用合金与纯钛参数的两温度模型仿真得到的峰值晶格温度差异仅约1.4%，而输运模型的函数形式（如是否采用低温Drude极限）影响可达19%，远大于元素与合金参数之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钛合金Ti-6Al-4V在超短脉冲激光烧蚀建模中一直被使用纯钛的电子输运参数，而缺乏针对合金本身的准确参数，这限制了预测精度。

Method: 采用Korringa–Kohn–Rostoker相干势近似（KKR-CPA）框架下的Kubo–Greenwood形式论，从第一性原理计算电子输运性质，同时处理化学无序和热无序，并与ABINIT电子-声子计算及实验数据比对验证。

Result: 纯钛的电阻率与实验和ABINIT计算一致，并复现了高温下Mott–Ioffe–Regel极限附近的饱和行为；Ti-6Al-4V的电子热导率最大值仅为纯钛的1/6.4（0.47 vs 2.97 kW/m/K）；两温度模型显示使用合金与纯钛参数仅产生1.4%的晶格温度差异，而采用低温Drude极限替代第一性原理热导率则导致19%的偏差。

Conclusion: 输运模型的函数形式（如是否考虑高温饱和效应）对预测精度的影响远大于使用元素还是合金参数的选择，因此改进输运模型的物理准确性比精确区分材料类型更为关键。

Abstract: Predictive modeling of ultrashort-pulse laser ablation requires temperature-dependent material parameters derived from the electronic structure, namely the electronic thermal conductivity, electron--phonon coupling, and heat capacity. These parameters are well documented for elemental metals but remain sparsely documented for alloys, apart from application-relevant exceptions such as stainless steels. The technologically important titanium alloy Ti-6Al-4V is a prominent example, which is still modeled using elemental-titanium values. We compute the electronic transport of hcp Ti and Ti-6Al-4V from first principles, using the Kubo--Greenwood formalism within the Korringa--Kohn--Rostoker coherent-potential-approximation framework, treating chemical and thermal disorder on equal footing. For elemental Ti, the calculated electrical resistivity agrees with independent \textsc{abinit} electron--phonon calculations and experiment, and also reproduces the high-temperature saturation near the Mott--Ioffe--Regel limit. Under electron--phonon nonequilibrium, the electronic thermal conductivity saturates and then decreases with electronic temperature, reaching a maximum of about \SI{2.97}{\kilo\watt\per\metre\per\kelvin} in Ti but only \SI{0.47}{\kilo\watt\per\metre\per\kelvin} in Ti-6Al-4V, a factor of 6.4 lower. In two-temperature-model simulations the alloy and elemental parameter sets yield peak lattice temperatures differing by only about 1.4\%, consistent with reported experimental ablation thresholds that differ by about 3\%, well within their measurement uncertainties. Replacing the first-principles thermal conductivity with the low-temperature Drude limit shifts the peak lattice temperature by up to 19\%, showing that the functional form of the transport model is even more important than the elemental vs alloy distinction for predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [128] [Correcting DFT formation energies towards experimental accuracy using foundational MLIPs and latent-feature delta-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18092)
*Timo Reents,Marnik Bercx,Giovanni Pizzi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Materials Cloud三维晶体数据库(MC3D)的热力学稳定性数据，并展示了如何利用基础机器学习原子间势能(MLIP)将形成能的平均绝对误差降低40%以上，再通过delta学习框架进一步将误差降至50 meV/atom以下，达到与实验不确定性相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量DFT计算得到的热力学稳定性数据（如形成能、凸包能量）对于指导新材料发现至关重要。现有数据库（如OQMD、MP）虽广泛应用，但存在计算成本高、与实验值存在系统性偏差等问题。本文旨在构建一个完全开源、可复现且以实验为中心的数据库，并探索利用基础MLIP提升形成能预测精度的方法。

Method: 1) 构建MC3D数据库并与OQMD、MP及实验形成焓进行比较。2) 使用基础MLIP（PET-OMATPES，r²SCAN级别）改进形成能预测，无需额外DFT计算。3) 在delta学习框架中，利用MLIP的潜在特征训练经典机器学习模型，进一步校正形成能。

Result: 1) 基础MLIP将形成能的平均绝对误差相对于GGA降低40%以上。2) delta学习模型将误差进一步降至50 meV/atom以下，与实验不确定性相当。3) 相比于仅使用成分特征，潜在特征结合适当正则化可同时降低预测误差并限制校正对相对相稳定性的影响。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了将PBEsol几何结构与meta-GGA能量结合的传统做法扩展到基础MLIP时代的可行性，并展示了利用MLIP潜在特征进行delta学习校正的有效性。MC3D数据库为材料发现提供了高质量、可复现的热力学稳定性基准。

Abstract: Crystal structure databases curated by high-throughput density functional theory calculations typically serve as the starting point for computational materials discovery efforts. Thermodynamic stability data, such as formation energies and the energy above the convex hull, are important quantities to guide the search for novel materials, enabling filtering for (meta)stable structures. Here, we present the thermodynamic stability of the fully open-source, reproducible, and experimentally focused Materials Cloud three-dimensional crystals database (MC3D). We compare against two other DFT databases, the Open Quantum Materials Database (OQMD) and the Materials Project (MP), as well as against experimental formation enthalpies. We then demonstrate how recent foundational machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) trained at the r$^2$SCAN level (specifically, we test PET-OMATPES here) can be leveraged to improve the agreement of formation energies with experiment, reducing the mean absolute error by more than 40% relative to GGA without requiring any additional DFT calculation. Our results validate and extend the established practice of combining PBEsol geometries with meta-GGA energies to the era of foundational MLIPs. Finally, we train classical machine learning models to further correct the formation energies in a delta-learning framework, where we use the information-rich latent features of the foundational MLIP. These models further reduce the mean absolute error below 50 meV/atom, bringing it down to values comparable with the experimental uncertainty itself. Notably, compared to purely compositional features, the latent features (combined with carefully tuned regularization) simultaneously reduce the prediction error and limit the impact of the learned corrections on the relative phase stability.

</details>


### [129] [Optimal transition states for polaron hopping transport without supercells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18096)
*Vasilii Vasilchenko,Matteo Giantomassi,Samuel Poncé,Xavier Gonze*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分极化子方程和字符串方法的超胞无关框架，用于从头计算极化子跳跃输运。该方法直接在倒易空间优化自陷极化子态之间的过渡态，无需超胞，成功应用于LiF和金红石TiO₂，揭示了多步和各向异性跳跃机制。在金红石TiO₂中，计算的电子-极化子迁移率与实验吻合，而玻尔兹曼输运理论显著高估了迁移率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DFT超胞方法计算极化子跳跃存在三个主要问题：极化子自相互作用、虚假静电效应以及随极化子尺寸增大而出现的可扩展性差。这些问题限制了第一性原理方法在极化子跳跃输运研究中的应用。

Method: 提出超胞无关框架，结合变分极化子方程（用于描述自陷极化子态）和字符串方法（用于优化过渡态），直接在倒易空间进行优化，获得沿跳跃路径的极化子构型。

Result: 应用于LiF和金红石TiO₂，揭示了多步和各向异性跳跃机制。在金红石TiO₂中，计算的电子-极化子迁移率与实验一致，而传统玻尔兹曼输运方法则显著高估了迁移率。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从第一性原理计算极化子跳跃动力学的可扩展路径，适用于电荷运动受自陷效应主导的材料体系。

Abstract: Polaron formation localizes charge carriers and drives a crossover from band-like to hopping transport in materials. Hopping dynamics can be obtained from DFT supercell calculations of transition states, but these suffer from polaron self-interaction, spurious electrostatics, and poor scaling with polaron size. We introduce a supercell-free framework for \textit{ab initio} polaron hopping transport based on variational polaron equations and the string method. The approach optimizes transition states between self-trapped polaron states directly in reciprocal space and provides the polaron configurations along the path, enabling evaluation of adiabatic hopping rates and mobilities. We apply the method to LiF and rutile TiO$_2$, revealing multi-step and anisotropic hopping mechanisms. In rutile TiO$_2$, the computed electron-polaron mobility agrees with experiment, whereas band-like Boltzmann transport substantially overestimates the mobility. Our results establish a scalable route to first-principles polaron-hopping dynamics in materials in which charge motion is governed by self-trapping.

</details>


### [130] [Defect configuration, not nitrogen content, governs the mechanical integrity of nitrogen-doped graphene: a molecular dynamics study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18129)
*Indranil Rudra,Jahid Emon,A. K. M. Monjur Morshed*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过分子动力学模拟，分离了氮掺杂石墨烯中不同氮构型（石墨氮、吡啶氮）及空位对力学性能的影响，发现力学退化主要由空位而非氮本身主导，且缺陷间的相互作用取决于它们相对于载荷方向的排列方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氮掺杂石墨烯的力学可靠性常归因于氮含量，但氮存在多种化学构型，各自的力学作用及与其他缺陷的相互作用尚不明确。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟对石墨烯进行单轴拉伸，对比三种尺寸匹配的缺陷：石墨氮簇、空位和吡啶氮簇，通过两组受控对照分离边缘化学和空位的独立效应。

Result: 石墨氮簇对强度影响小（<1%），空位降低强度约23%，吡啶氮簇最严重（~30%）。氮簇与空位共存时，在线排列的缺陷相互作用可忽略，而并列排列的缺陷通过应力场耦合相互影响，该作用持续到~80 Å的间距。

Conclusion: 氮改性石墨烯的力学完整性由缺陷构型、氮的键合环境以及共存缺陷相对于载荷的排列决定，而非仅由氮含量或缺陷密度决定，为耐缺陷设计提供了基础。

Abstract: The mechanical reliability of nitrogen-doped graphene is often attributed to its nitrogen content, yet nitrogen occurs in chemically distinct configurations whose individual mechanical roles, and whose interactions with other defects, remain unresolved. Here, molecular dynamics simulations of uniaxial tension are used to separate the contributions of nitrogen chemistry, missing atoms, and defect arrangement to the strength and fracture of graphene. Three size-matched defects, a graphitic-nitrogen cluster, a void, and a pyridinic-nitrogen cluster, are compared so that two controlled contrasts isolate the effects of edge chemistry and of the vacancy independently. The graphitic cluster leaves the mechanical properties essentially unchanged (a strength reduction of <1 %), whereas the void degrades the ultimate strength by ~23 % and the pyridinic cluster, which combines the same vacancy with edge nitrogen, is the most damaging (~30 %), failing abruptly from its nitrogen-decorated rim rather than through the damage-tolerant process of the bare void. The mechanical impact of a nitrogen cluster is therefore governed by whether it carries vacancies, not by nitrogen itself. When a nitrogen cluster and a void coexist, their interaction is controlled by orientation relative to the load: in-line defects interact negligibly and fail at the more severe member, whereas side-by-side defects couple through overlapping stress fields and weaken the sheet progressively as they approach, an interaction that persists to separations of ~80 Å. These results establish that the mechanical integrity of nitrogen-modified graphene is determined by the configuration of defects, the bonding environment of nitrogen, and the arrangement of coexisting defects relative to the load, rather than by nitrogen content or defect density alone, thereby providing a basis for defect-tolerant design.

</details>


### [131] [Phase-field modeling of TiO2 nanocarving via reaction with hydrogen-bearing gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18172)
*Alireza Seifi,Sheikh Akbar,Yanzhou Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究构建了二维相场模型，系统揭示了TiO2纳米刻蚀过程中反应速率各向异性是形成[001]纳米线阵列的主导因素，并建立了晶粒取向控制图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现利用还原性H2气体刻蚀TiO2块体多晶可制备[001]纳米线阵列，但强各向异性纳米线形貌的形成机制尚不明确。

Method: 构建了包含TiO2还原反应、Ti³⁺扩散以及表面能、扩散率、反应速率各向异性的二维相场模型，分别在单晶和多晶体系中进行系统模拟。

Result: 识别出反应速率各向异性是实验观察到的纳米线形貌的主导因素；探究了晶粒取向差的影响，生成了纳米刻蚀形貌图谱。

Conclusion: 本研究深化了对纳米刻蚀过程中微观结构演化机制的理解，为相关微观结构控制提供了理论指导。

Abstract: TiO2 nanocrystals can be fabricated by carving the TiO2 bulk polycrystals using reductive H2-bearing gases, yielding single-crystal [001] nanowire arrays. However, the origin of the strongly anisotropic nanowire morphology during nanocarving remains largely unexplored. In this study, we formulate a 2-D phase-field model to investigate the TiO2 morphology evolution during nanocarving processes. The model incorporates TiO2 reduction reaction, Ti3+ diffusion, and anisotropies in surface energy, diffusivity and reaction rate. Through systematic simulations in both single- and poly-crystals, we elucidate the roles of different anisotropy factors in nanocrystal morphologies at different carving stages, identifying the strong reaction rate anisotropy as the dominant factor for experimentally observed nanowire morphologies. We further explore the effect of grain misorientation angle, generating a nanocarving morphology map to guide grain orientation control. This study provides insights into microstructure evolution mechanisms during nanocarving and guidances for related microstructure control.

</details>


### [132] [Machine Learning Potential-Driven Molecular Dynamics Simulations of Dehydrogenation in Pristine and Doped MgH$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18182)
*Bo Han,Jianchuan Wang,Rui Zhang,Martin Matas,Elias Vigl,Jing Tang,Yong Du,Christian Weiss,David Holec*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习势驱动的分子动力学模拟（ML-MD），从原子尺度揭示了原始与掺杂MgH₂的脱氢动力学。发现MgH₂(100)面是最活跃的低指数面，并揭示了一种新的H₂生成机制——H₂在次表层生成后扩散至表面脱附。通过22种元素筛选，确定Ni为最佳掺杂剂，并建立Miedema电子密度（nws）与释氢量的火山型关系（最优窗口4.0-5.4×10⁻² e/bohr³），为催化相的“氢泵”效应提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原子尺度理解原始及掺杂MgH₂的脱氢动力学机制，筛选高效掺杂剂，建立定量构效关系以指导高性能储氢催化剂的设计。

Method: 采用机器学习势驱动的分子动力学模拟（ML-MD），系统研究不同表面取向，筛选22种掺杂元素，结合机器学习分析识别关键描述符（时间耦合Miedema电子密度），建立火山型关系。

Result: MgH₂(100)面为最活跃的低指数面；发现次表层H₂生成-扩散脱附的新机制；Ni为最佳掺杂剂；Miedema电子密度nws是关键描述符，最优窗口4.0-5.4×10⁻² e/bohr³；掺杂剂兼具热力学H吸引与适中相互作用以促进H-H耦合的双重功能。

Conclusion: 从原子层面验证了催化相的“氢泵”效应，展示了ML-MD在解析复杂催化机制和建立定量活性关系方面的强大能力，为MgH₂及其他储氢材料的高性能催化剂理性设计提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: Machine learning potential-driven molecular dynamics simulations (ML-MD) were employed to provide atomistic insights into the dehydrogenation kinetics of pristine and doped MgH2. Through systematic investigation of distinct surface orientations, the MgH2 (100) surface was identified as the most active low-index surface for hydrogen release. For pristine MgH2, our simulations revealed a novel H2 formation mechanism characterized by H2 generation in the subsurface region followed by diffusion to the surface for desorption, highlighting the critical role of subsurface processes beyond conventional surface-driven pathways. Comprehensive screening of 22 doping elements identified Ni as the most effective dopant. Among several descriptors, machine learning analysis identified the time-coupled Miedema electron density as the critical descriptor, underscoring the role of electronic properties. Consequently, a volcano-shaped relationship was uncovered between the intrinsic Miedema electron density ( nws ) and total hydrogen release (optimal window: 4.0 < nws < 5.4x10-2 e/bohr3). Dopants within this range serve a dual function: acting as thermodynamic sinks for H attraction while maintaining a balanced interaction strength to facilitate H-H coupling and H2 release. This atomistic-level validation provides strong theoretical support for the experimentally observed "hydrogen pump" effect of catalytic phases. The present study demonstrates the strong capability of ML- MD in navigating through complex catalytic mechanisms and establishing quantitative property-activity relationships, providing a robust framework for rational design of high-performance catalysts for MgH2 and other hydrogen storage materials.

</details>


### [133] [Excitonic effects in the photocarriers dynamics of two-dimensional materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18183)
*Carlos Betancur,Gianluca Stefanucci,Enrico Perfetto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于激子Bloch方程（XBE）的双粒子框架，用于描述光激发载流子的超快动力学。与忽略电子-空穴关联的单粒子方法不同，XBE明确考虑了激子形成。在WSe₂单层中的计算表明，XBE预测了增强的谷间散射和Q谷占主导的载流子分布，与时间分辨ARPES实验一致，且热化载流子的动量分布受激子波函数而非费米-狄拉克统计调控，标志着关联非平衡态的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单粒子框架（如电子-电子和电子-声子散射）忽略了能带边附近占主导的电子-空穴关联（激子效应），无法准确描述半导体中光激发载流子的超快弛豫动力学。

Method: 引入基于激子Bloch方程（XBE）的双粒子框架，该框架同时捕捉载流子-声子散射和激子形成过程，并将其应用于非共振光激发的WSe₂单层体系。

Result: 与现有方法相比，XBE预测了增强的谷间散射以及Q谷中载流子占优的分布（而非K谷），与时间分辨ARPES实验吻合；热化载流子的动量分布由激子波函数主导，而非费米-狄拉克统计。

Conclusion: 激子关联是调控激子材料中光载流子动力学的关键机制，传统单粒子图像对这类体系的描述存在根本性局限。

Abstract: We investigate the role of excitonic correlations in shaping the ultrafast dynamics of photoexcited carriers in semiconductors. Conventional approaches describe relaxation within single-particle frameworks, where electron-electron and electron-phonon scattering drive thermalization toward Fermi-Dirac distributions, neglecting electron-hole correlations that dominate near band edges. We introduce a two-particle framework based on excitonic Bloch equations (XBE) that captures carrier-phonon scattering and explicitly accounts for exciton formation. Applying this approach to non-resonantly photoexcited WSe$_2$ monolayers, we reveal qualitatively different carrier relaxation pathways: in contrast to state-of-the-art methods, XBE predict enhanced intervalley scattering and dominant carrier population in Q valleys over K valleys, in agreement with time-resolved ARPES experiments. Moreover, the momentum distribution of thermalized carriers is shaped by exciton wavefunctions rather than by Fermi-Dirac statistics, signaling the formation of a correlated nonequilibrium state. These results establish excitonic correlations as a key mechanism governing photocarrier dynamics in excitonic materials.

</details>
